# ZIVO — Agent task board

Shared to-do list for **Claude + Codex + DeepSeek**. Claim work here before you start so
the other two don't touch the same files. Rules: [`docs/agent-workflow.md`](docs/agent-workflow.md).

**How to use:** move a line between sections and add `— @agent, YYYY-MM-DD`.
One agent per file/page. Run `npm run update` before moving anything to **Done**.

## In review

- **LiveLocationsPage premium interaction + a11y pass** (`src/pages/LiveLocationsPage.tsx` [reached by in-app navigation, 182 lines, `useAuth` + `SwipeBackContainer` + `SEOHead noIndex`, NO bottom nav]) — customer-facing **active live-location shares manager** (view + stop your running location shares): reads the real `live_locations` table (key `["live-locations-me", user?.id]`, `.select("id, user_id, chat_kind, chat_key, latitude, longitude, accuracy_m, expires_at, updated_at")`, `.eq("user_id", user.id)`, `.order("updated_at", { ascending: false })`, `enabled: !!user?.id`, `staleTime 15_000`); `stopShare(id)` = **optimistic** `qc.setQueryData` (filter the row out) → `supabase…from("live_locations").delete().eq("id", id)` → `toast.success("Stopped sharing")` (onError `toast.error` + `invalidateQueries`); `stats` `useMemo` (total/active/groups/directs over non-expired shares); `formatRemaining`/`formatRelative` pure helpers. Layout: sticky header (shadcn back `<Button>` + MapPin badge + "Live Locations" title), a gradient hero stat card (`{stats.active} active` + directs/groups breakdown, `motion.div` NO `onClick`), loading skeletons + empty-state card, then a vertical list of **share rows** (each a presentational `motion.div` [entrance anim + `cn()` expired/active border treatment, NO `onClick`]: chat-kind icon tile + "direct/group chat" label + Live/Expired badge + lat/long `font-mono` coords + chat-key/time-remaining meta + a CONDITIONAL trailing "Stop" action button shown only when `!expired`). **1 control / 1 edit (1 in-place className change, 0 new attrs)**, all className-only; `git diff` = **1 ins / 1 del (+0 net)**. **Inventory exhaustive (full line-by-line read) — exactly 1 RAW `<button type="button">` (the Stop button, the page's ONLY non-shadcn control), 0 `motion.button`, 1 shadcn `<Button>` (back); 0 RAW `<input>`.** **(1) Stop button** (L165, RAW, conditionally rendered ONLY when `!expired`, ALREADY `aria-label="Stop sharing"`, `onClick={() => stopShare(s.id)}`, visible child = a decorative `Square` icon [`fill="currentColor"`] + the text "Stop", was `className="shrink-0 h-9 px-3 rounded-full bg-rose-500/15 hover:bg-rose-500/25 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors"`) → **FLIP `transition-colors`→`transition-all`** + appended `active:scale-[0.98] focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring`; **FLIP required** — the existing `transition-colors` eases the `hover:bg-rose-500/25` color but does NOT cover the new `active:scale` **transform**, so `transition-all` (superset) eases both the hover-bg AND the press-scale; **medium chip/pill tier → `active:scale-[0.98]`** (a compact `h-9 px-3 rounded-full` pill with an icon + short text — NOT icon-only [→ not `[0.95]`], NOT full-width [→ not `[0.99]`], NOT a segmented filter chip [→ not `[0.97]`]); **KEPT `aria-label="Stop sharing"`** (valid pre-existing accessible name; the visible word "Stop" is a substring of "Stop sharing" → WCAG 2.5.3 "label in name" satisfied → don't-churn says keep it, not strip it); **NO `aria-pressed`** (one-shot destructive action — optimistic delete + toast — NOT a persistent on/off toggle); **OUTWARD ring** — the button is the trailing `shrink-0` child of its parent share-row `motion.div` (L141, `cn()` includes `rounded-2xl bg-card border` with `p-3` padding, NOT `overflow-hidden`) → the `p-3` (12px) clearance keeps an outward 2px ring well clear of the row's rounded corners → no clip → no `ring-inset`. **SKIPPED:** shadcn back `<Button aria-label="Back" variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full" onClick={navigate(-1)}>` (L99, ships tokens, labeled); hero stat `motion.div` (L112, entrance `initial/animate` only, NO `onClick` → presentational); each share-row `motion.div` (L141, entrance anim + expired/active `cn()` border, NO `onClick` → presentational — only the Stop button inside ACTIVE rows is a control); loading-skeleton `div`s (L124) + empty-state card `div` (L126) non-interactive; MapPin/Users/Clock/Hash/Square/Sparkles icons + the "Live"/"Expired" badge `<span>`s + the lat/long/coords `<p>` (display children, decorative). **Advisors DeepSeek + MiMo both consulted** (`docs/agent-runs/slice36-livelocations-{deepseek,mimo}.md`); **perfect three-way convergence — both completed (exit 0), neither truncated, byte-identical final className string + identical diffstat (1 ins / 1 del, +0 net), ZERO corrections to every Q1–Q4.** Both independently confirmed: (Q1) the treatment (FLIP `transition-colors`→`transition-all` + `active:scale-[0.98]` + OUTWARD ring, KEEP existing `aria-label`, NO `aria-pressed`) is correct + consistent; (Q2) `active:scale-[0.98]` (medium chip/pill) is the right tier for this compact icon+text pill (not icon-only `[0.95]`, not full-width `[0.99]`); (Q3) OUTWARD ring is right (the button has `p-3` clearance inside a non-`overflow-hidden` row → no clip); (Q4) the untouched list is correct + at 375px the share row (icon tile + chat-kind/coords/meta + trailing Stop pill) lays out without crushing (~209-219px for the middle column; coords + meta wrap naturally via `flex-wrap` + `min-w-0`). **HARD RULE honored — every `onClick`/`stopShare(s.id)`/the optimistic `qc.setQueryData` + `delete().eq("id", id)` + `toast` + `invalidateQueries`/`navigate(-1)`/`useQuery` (key `["live-locations-me", user?.id]`)/`supabase…from("live_locations")` select/eq/order/`useMemo` (`stats`)/`formatRemaining`/`formatRelative`/the `expired` computation/the `!expired` conditional render left byte-identical** — a guard grep over the diff shows exactly ONE `−`/`+` pair (the Stop button's className string, className-only); the `type="button"`, `aria-label="Stop sharing"`, and `onClick={() => stopShare(s.id)}` sit on SEPARATE lines (the button is multi-line JSX), ABSENT from the diff = provably untouched. Diff counts verified: **1 flip (transition-colors→transition-all), 1 `active:scale-[0.98]`, 1 OUTWARD ring, 0 aria added/removed, 0 aria-pressed, 0 `ring-inset`.** **Responsive 375/768/1280 — clean (both advisors, with the math):** `max-w-2xl mx-auto px-4` → ~343px inner at 375px; header back `h-10 w-10` (40px) + MapPin badge + title fits; hero `p-5` `text-3xl "{N} active"` + directs/groups line; **share row** `flex items-start gap-3 p-3` → chat-kind icon tile `h-10 w-10` (40px) + `gap-3` (12px) fixed left, trailing Stop pill `h-9 px-3` (~60-70px) fixed right, `flex-1 min-w-0` middle column (chat-kind label + Live/Expired badge `flex-wrap`, coords `font-mono` line, chat-key/time-remaining meta) → **~209px for the middle column → no crush** (coords/meta wrap via `flex-wrap` + `min-w-0`). **No keyboard gaps to flag** — the Stop button is a real `<button>` (natively focusable; the added outward ring is functional, not dead CSS; `aria-label` already announces "Stop sharing"); the back shadcn `<Button>` ships its own tokens; the hero + share-row `motion.div`s have no `onClick`. **SOFT OWNER FLAG (logic, out of className+display-only scope — do NOT fix here):** `stopShare` has NO `confirm()` guard (the DevicesPage Remove twin DID use `confirm()`) — stopping a live-location share is an instant optimistic delete on a single click/Enter; it IS low-stakes + reversible (re-share from the chat attachment menu) + toast-confirmed, so this is a minor note, but the owner may want a confirm step for parity with the device-removal flow. **Owner flag (established repo compact pattern, not a per-page fix):** sub-44px tap targets — Stop `h-9` ~36px, back `h-10 w-10` ~40px (the share row is a tall full-width container = fine). **Verified** `npm run update` green — **TRUE EXIT 0** (re-verified with a clean unpiped zsh-safe `$?` after an initial run hit the known transient `injectManifest` collision [vite-plugin-pwa "Unable to find a place to inject the manifest… swSrc and swDest configured to the same file" at `closeBundle` — a parallel agent's concurrent build clobbering `dist/sw.js` mid-flight, NOT a type/code error: both type-checks had passed and BOTH the client `✓ built in 17.80s` AND worker SW `✓ built in 61ms` compiled before the PWA manifest-injection step]; retried behind the `until ! pgrep -f 'vite/bin/vite.js build'` guard → clean): client `✓ built in 6.94s`, worker SW `✓ built in 21ms`, `dist/sw.js` freshly generated + **PWA precache 18 entries (2551.64 KiB)**; - **CheckInPage premium interaction + a11y pass** (`src/pages/CheckInPage.tsx` [reached by in-app navigation, 175 lines, `useAuth`]) — customer-facing **location check-in social feature**: `loadCheckins` (`useCallback`) reads `check_ins` (`.select("id, user_id, location_name, caption, created_at, is_public")`, `.eq("is_public", true)`, `.order("created_at", { ascending: false })`, `.limit(20)`) into a local `checkins` `useState` (mapped to a view-model; `likes`/`comments` hardcoded `0`, `taggedFriends` `[]`); `handleCheckIn` `.insert(...)` a new check-in + `toast` + reload; local `useState` holds `showCreate`/`location`/`caption`/`tagInput`/`loading`/`submitting`. Layout: sticky header (shadcn back `<Button>` + MapPin + "Check-in" title + shadcn "Check in" toggle `<Button>`), a collapsible create-form (`motion.div` + 3 shadcn `<Input>`s + shadcn submit/Photo/Cancel `<Button>`s), then a vertical list of **check-in cards** (each `motion.div` [entrance anim, NO `onClick`] wrapping a shadcn `<Card>` = avatar + name/time + location + caption + tagged-friends + a Like/Comment action row). **2 controls / 2 edits (both fold `aria-label` + className tokens inline onto the existing single-line buttons)**, all className + display-only-attr; `git diff` = **2 ins / 2 del (+0 net)**. **Inventory exhaustive (full line-by-line read) — exactly 2 RAW `<button type="button">` (the Like + Comment buttons, the page's ONLY non-shadcn controls), 0 `motion.button`, 6 shadcn `<Button>` + 3 shadcn `<Input>` + 1 shadcn `<Card>` (all ship tokens).** **⚠️ THE CENTRAL JUDGMENT CALL — both RAW buttons are DEAD STUBS (no `onClick`; clicking does nothing today; their only visible child text is a bare count number; no focus ring, no `aria-label`).** I posed **3 schools** to both advisors — School A (full treatment incl press-scale), School B (leave dead stubs untouched + owner-flag only), School C (a11y baseline ONLY: ring + `aria-label`, NO press-scale, + owner-flag). **Both advisors INDEPENDENTLY converged on School C.** **(1) Like button** (L162, RAW icon+count `Heart` + `{checkin.likes}`, was `className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs hover:text-red-500 transition-colors"` [NO `onClick`, NO ring, NO `aria-label`]) → added `aria-label="Like"` + appended `focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring`; **KEPT `transition-colors`** (NO flip); **NO `active:scale`**. **(2) Comment button** (L165, RAW icon+count `MessageCircle` + `{checkin.comments}`, was `className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs hover:text-blue-500 transition-colors"`) → added `aria-label="Comment"` + same ring append; **KEPT `transition-colors`**; **NO `active:scale`**. **School-C rationale (both advisors):** the buttons ARE real `<button>`s → a keyboard user WILL Tab to them and currently gets NO focus indicator (a genuine a11y gap TODAY, independent of the missing `onClick`), AND their accessible name is just a bare count number `0` (meaningless to a screen reader — another genuine gap); both fixed by ring + `aria-label`. BUT a press-scale (`active:scale`) on a dead stub would manufacture a NEW false "press-me-and-something-happens" tactile affordance the code doesn't currently have — pressing and getting nothing is worse UX than no feedback — so NO scale until the owner wires `onClick` (at which point upgrade to School A). **`aria-label` IS warranted here** (the token rule: a control whose only visible child text is a bare number/count gets an `aria-label`, since a count alone is not a meaningful accessible name). **OUTWARD ring** — both buttons sit inside a shadcn `<Card className="p-4">` via `<div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-muted-foreground">`; the Card is NOT `overflow-hidden` and the `p-4` (16px) clearance is well beyond a 2px ring → no `ring-inset`. **The one divergence (resolved):** DeepSeek proposed FLIPPING `transition-colors`→`transition-all` (calling it a "harmless superset"); MiMo KEPT `transition-colors` ("don't-churn — no new animated property"). **I applied MiMo's keep-`transition-colors`** because (a) no `active:scale`/transform is being added, so nothing NEW needs easing → the don't-churn rule says keep the valid existing value; (b) `focus-visible:ring-2` is a **box-shadow**, which is NOT in the `transition-colors` property list → with `transition-colors` the focus ring appears **instantly** (the conventional, accessible behavior), whereas `transition-all` would make the ring FADE in (slightly worse); DeepSeek itself flagged its flip as merely "harmless," not necessary. **SKIPPED:** shadcn back `<Button aria-label="Back" variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={navigate(-1)}>` (L92, ships tokens, labeled); shadcn "Check in" toggle `<Button size="sm" onClick={setShowCreate(!showCreate)}>` (L96, ships tokens, visible text); the create-form `motion.div` (L102, entrance anim, NO `onClick` → presentational wrapper); the 3 shadcn `<Input>`s (L107/109/112, ship tokens); shadcn form submit `<Button onClick={handleCheckIn} disabled={...}>` (L115) + Cancel `<Button onClick={setShowCreate(false)}>` (L119) (ship tokens, visible text); each check-in card `motion.div` (L141, entrance anim, NO `onClick` → presentational); the shadcn `<Card className="p-4">` (L142, non-clickable container); Avatar/MapPin/Clock/Users/Heart/MessageCircle icons + all `<span>`/`<p>` text decorative; loading skeletons + empty-state non-interactive. **⚠️ OWNER FLAGS (out of className+display-only scope — need LOGIC):** (1) the **Like + Comment buttons are dead stubs** (no `onClick`; `likes`/`comments` hardcoded to `0` in the `loadCheckins` view-model map) — owner to wire the like/comment actions + fetch real counts, then upgrade these to School A (add `active:scale-[0.97]` + flip to `transition-all`); (2) the shadcn **"Photo" `<Button>`** (L118, `variant="outline"`, visible text "Photo") is ALSO a dead stub (no `onClick`) — left className-UNCHANGED (shadcn ships its own tokens, same owner-flag pattern as CouponsPage's Filter `<Button>` dead stub) — owner to wire camera/upload or remove. Plus the established repo compact-pattern flag: sub-44px tap targets — Like/Comment inline `text-xs` rows, back `size="icon"`. **Advisors DeepSeek + MiMo both consulted** (`docs/agent-runs/slice34-checkin-{deepseek,mimo}.md`); **three-way convergence on School C — both completed (exit 0), neither truncated; both independently picked School C with the SAME reasoning (real focusable buttons → focus-ring + accessible-name gaps are real today, but no fake press-scale on a no-op), both confirmed OUTWARD ring + the full SKIP list + the Photo dead-stub owner-flag + 375px clean; the ONLY delta was the `transition-colors` keep-vs-flip, resolved to MiMo's keep (don't-churn + instant-ring).** **HARD RULE honored — every `onClick` (none on the 2 edited buttons — they're stubs)/`loadCheckins`/`handleCheckIn`/`supabase…from("check_ins")` select/eq/order/limit/insert/`navigate(-1)`/`setShowCreate`/`useState`/`useEffect`/`useCallback`/`toast` left byte-identical** — a guard grep over the diff shows exactly 2 `−`/`+` pairs (the Like + Comment button opening tags); each `+` preserves `type="button"` + the `hover:text-*` + `transition-colors` **byte-identical**, adding ONLY `aria-label` + the ring tokens (no `onClick` existed on these lines to disturb); a file-grep confirms `loadCheckins`/`handleCheckIn` defs + the `check_ins` query/insert all intact. Diff counts verified: **2 rings added, 2 `aria-label` added, 0 `active:scale`, 0 flip, 0 `ring-inset`.** **Responsive 375/768/1280 — clean (both advisors, with the math):** header back `size="icon"` (40px) + MapPin + "Check-in" title + flex-1 + "Check in" `size="sm" rounded-full` toggle fits (~276px of 343); create-form button row `flex gap-2` (submit + Photo + Cancel ≈ 226px) fits; check-in card `p-4` → ~311px usable: avatar `h-8 w-8` (32px) + `gap-2` + `flex-1 min-w-0` name/time, location row, caption (`SafeCaption` wraps), **Like/Comment row** `flex items-center gap-4` → Heart(12px)+count + `gap-4` + MessageCircle(12px)+count ≈ ~52px → no crush. **No keyboard gaps to flag on the live controls** — both Like/Comment are now real focusable `<button>`s with a functional ring + accessible name; the back/toggle/form shadcn `<Button>`s + `<Input>`s ship their own tokens; the card `motion.div`s have no `onClick`. **Verified** `npm run update` green — **TRUE EXIT 0** (clean zsh-safe `$?`, unpiped): worker SW built + `dist/sw.js` freshly generated + **PWA precache 18 entries (2551.64 KiB)**; both type-checks pass (build runs only on `&&`). _Preview caveat: CheckInPage reads the live `check_ins` table (`is_public` rows render the cards, else the "No check-ins yet" empty-state; the Like/Comment buttons mount per card; the create-form mounts on the "Check in" toggle), and the shared myzivo-dev server is contended by parallel agents → per the established preview-contention protocol I relied on the green gate + the guard-clean 2/2 `aria-label`+className-only diff + the **byte-identical** token pattern (RAW icon+count control with no meaningful name: `aria-label` + ring, keep `transition-colors`, no `active:scale` on a dead stub)._ — @claude, 2026-06-13

press-scale is the only animated prop → `transition-transform`, no `hover:bg` on this button). **(2) tabs ×3** — RAW `<button>` `.map` (visible text+icon, `transition-colors` present, selection only via `bg-ig-gradient` vs `bg-muted/50`, `rounded-full`, parent `flex gap-1 px-4` NOT overflow-hidden) → `aria-pressed={activeTab === t.id}` + appended `active:scale-[0.97]` + ring to the `cn()` base, `transition-colors`→**`transition-all`** (so the new scale eases alongside the bg-swap); **outward** ring (segmented tier; visible text = accessible name → no aria-label). **(3) search-result user rows** — RAW wide `<button>` (avatar + name, `hover:bg-accent/50 transition-colors`, parent `p-4 space-y-2` NOT overflow-hidden, button IS the rounded element) → appended `active:scale-[0.99]` + **outward** ring + `transition-colors`→**`transition-all`** (wide-row tier; rich text → no aria-label). **(4) trending grid tiles** — `motion.button` w/ `whileTap={{ scale: 0.97 }}` (CSS `active:scale` DEAD → motion owns press) image-only `<img alt="">` in a `grid grid-cols-3 gap-0.5` (2px gutter) → **ring only** appended into the **FIRST `cn()` base string** + `aria-label="Open post"`, **with `ring-inset`** (a normal 2px outward ring would bleed into the tight `gap-0.5` gutter and touch neighbors → inset sits crisply on the image edge; an element's own `overflow-hidden` does NOT clip its own box-shadow ring, but inset is the right call for gutter-bleed). **(5) suggested-people rows + hashtag rows** — two RAW wide `<button>` `.map`s sharing a **byte-identical** className (`…hover:bg-accent/50 transition-colors text-left`) → single `replace_all` → appended `active:scale-[0.99]` + ring + `transition-all` (wide-row tier; no aria-label). **(6) "Clear" hashtag text button** — RAW `<button>` visible text "Clear" + `X` icon (`text-xs … hover:text-foreground transition-colors`, not rounded) → appended `active:scale-[0.97] rounded-sm` + ring + `transition-all` (small inline text-link tier; **ADDED `rounded-sm`** for clean ring radius; visible text → no aria-label). **(7) tagged grid tiles** — `motion.button` w/ `whileTap={{ scale: 0.97 }}` image-only, same `grid-cols-3 gap-0.5` → **ring only** + `aria-label="Open post"`, **with `ring-inset`** (same gutter-bleed reasoning as #4). **SKIPPED:** search `<input>` (already `focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary/30` → leave alone); `DegradedDataBanner`/`LoadFailureCard` (separate components, retry buttons internal via `onRetry` props — no raw JSX button here); `PullToRefresh` wrapper; `ZivoMobileNav`; `Avatar`; `VerifiedBadge` (`interactive={false}`); `Loader2` spinners; `<img>`/`<video>` media. **HARD RULE:** zero changes to supabase queries/react-query keys, `navigate('/profile/…'|'/reels/…')` targets, `setSearch`/`setActiveTab`/`setSelectedTag` onClick bodies, filter/`isLarge`/`i % 7` logic — className + 3 display-only attrs only (guard-grep over the diff confirmed every `+`/`−` pair is a className string or a standalone `aria-*` line). **Three-way convergence (DeepSeek + MiMo + my audit), ZERO corrections:** both advisors confirmed all 7 control groups + all 5 open Qs — Q1 keep the clear-X ring (`active:scale-95`+`transition-transform`); Q2 **`ring-inset`** for the image tiles (gutter-bleed avoidance — both endorsed); Q3 static **`aria-label="Open post"`** on both tile maps; Q4 **`aria-pressed`** (toggle-button semantics, NOT `role="tab"`/tablist — no `aria-controls`/tabpanel wiring; matches the repo segmented-filter precedent); Q5 375px clean. _[Caught + corrected a DeepSeek slip: its trending-tile diff appended the ring as a NEW `cn()` argument with NO comma after `isLarge && "col-span-2 row-span-2"` → two adjacent string literals = SyntaxError; the applied version instead appends the ring tokens into the FIRST `cn()` base string — MiMo's independent diff did exactly this, confirming the fix.]_ **Responsive 375px — clean (both advisors):** 3-tab row (`flex gap-1 px-4`, ~55px/chip ×3 ≈180px in 343px inner) comfortable; `grid-cols-3 gap-0.5` media grid ~124px/col (the `col-span-2 row-span-2` large tile every 7th item ~249px fits); full-width rows fine. **Gate:** `npm run update` **TRUE EXIT 0** (unpiped), client `✓ built in 10.29s`, worker SW `✓ built in 31ms`, `dist/sw.js` freshly generated + PWA precache 18 entries (2551.41 KiB); both type-checks pass (build runs only on `&&`). **Advisor docs:** `docs/agent-runs/slice21-explore-{deepseek,mimo}.md`. **Preview:** data-gated (search needs `>1` char + matching `public_profiles`; trending/tagged grids need populated posts; hashtags need rows) + shared myzivo-dev server contended by parallel agents → per the established preview-contention protocol relied on the green gate + the guard-clean 11/8 className-only diff + byte-identical token patterns proven **live** on prior slices (icon-only `active:scale-95`+ring; segmented `aria-pressed` `active:scale-[0.97]`+ring like the Achievements/Challenges filter tabs; wide-row `active:scale-[0.99]`+ring; `motion.button` `whileTap` ring-only + `ring-inset` image grid). **Owner flags (NOT fixed — logic/content, out of scope):** clear-X ~16px + tabs ~30px tall = sub-44px tap targets (established repo compact pattern); no arrow-key roving-tabindex grid navigation on the media tiles; no empty-state when `trendingPosts` is empty + not-loading (silent blank grid); the suggested-people query's `is_of_creator=false` filter may exclude creators from "Suggested for you" — flag in case it's an unintended exclusion. — @claude, 2026-06-13

the only interactive child is the per-card RSVP `<button>`, which IS keyboard-reachable, so this isn't a focus trap — but users can't open an event's detail from here); owner fix: add `onClick={() => navigate(\`/events/${event.id}\`)}` + `role="button"` + `tabIndex={0}` + `onKeyDown` (or wrap in `<Link>`), then a `focus-visible:ring` in a follow-up — same flag-not-fix precedent as the CommunitiesPage cards (Slice 19). The two `replace_all` targets are **disjoint** (`text-primary underline` ≠ `text-primary/60 underline` — the `/60` before the space prevents any cross-match) → order-independent; the `support@zivosmedia.com` `<span className="text-primary font-semibold">` shares no string and stays untouched. **Open questions resolved (both advisors agreed):** (a) **back-button transition → LEAVE it** (a hover-bg transition is a visual change beyond a token pass; the ring is instant/a11y-correct); (b) **footer `text-[9px]` "See also" links → IN SCOPE** (genuine navigational `<a>`s with zero focus-visible ring — the same a11y gap the standard fixes everywhere; tiny size doesn't exempt keyboard accessibility). **HARD RULE honored — every `onClick={() => navigate(-1)}` / every `<Link to=…>` target (`/grocery/terms`,`/grocery/fees`,`/legal/privacy`,`/legal/terms`,`/grocery/returns`) / the `FEE_BREAKDOWN`/`PRICE_PROMISES`/`CANCEL_FEES`/`REFUND_TABLE`/`STEPS`/`sections` content arrays / the `@/config/groceryPricing` imports (`formatFee`/`calcMarkup`/`calcServiceFee`/`getMarkupPct`/`DELIVERY_*`/`SERVICE_FEE_*`/`MARKUP_THRESHOLD`/`TIP_OPTIONS`) / the example-order total math / `useNavigate` left byte-identical**: combined `git diff` = exactly **7 insertions / 7 deletions** (GroceryFees 1/1, GroceryReturns 2/2, GroceryTerms 4/4 — the 4/4 because the 2 intro `<Link>`s sit on 2 separate physical lines), and a guard grep over the diff (`navigate`/`onClick`/`to=`/`formatFee`/`calcMarkup`/`calcServiceFee`/`DELIVERY_`/`SERVICE_FEE_`/`MARKUP_THRESHOLD`/`REFUND_TABLE`/`FEE_BREAKDOWN`/`PRICE_PROMISES`/`CANCEL_FEES`/`STEPS`/`sections`/`exampleTotal`/`useNavigate`) surfaced only the back-button lines (`navigate(-1)` byte-identical on both `-`/`+` sides) and the `<Link>` lines (every `to=` target byte-identical, only className extended) → **CLEAN**. GroceryReturns refund `grid grid-cols-3` ≈114px/col, longest issue "Poor quality produce/meat" wraps to 2 lines at word boundaries (cells grow in height, **no horizontal overflow**), how-to grid is `sm:grid-cols-2` → single column at 375px; GroceryTerms `whitespace-pre-line` section cards are plain word-wrapping text at 12px in `px-4`, no long unbreakable tokens → no overflow. **(B) `support@zivosmedia.com`** is a plain `<span className="text-primary font-semibold">` on **all 3 pages** (NOT a `mailto:` link) — identical across the trio, **looks intentional**; converting to `mailto:` is a behavior/content change out of scope. **Flag only.** **(C) Contrast** — the `text-[9px]` footer links at `text-primary/60` (+ the `text-muted-foreground/50` lead-in) are below WCAG AA at that size, but this is **consistent micro-footer styling** (de-emphasized secondary nav, same on all 3 pages), not a regression; an owner could bump to `text-primary/70`+`text-muted-foreground/60` for AA but that's an aesthetic/a11y call beyond the token pass. **Flag only.** **Verified** `npm run update` green (**true exit 0, unpiped** — type-check + worker type-check both pass since the build only runs on `&&`; the GroceryOrderHistory header back/refresh), and small-text-link `active:scale-[0.97]`+`rounded-sm`+ring (the CoinPurchaseSuccess "Go to Wallet instead" + BotPublicProfilePage "create your own" links)._ — @claude, 2026-06-13

**(5) star-rating `motion.button` ×5** (`whileTap={{ scale: 0.8 }}`, icon-only `<Star>`) → added **dynamic `aria-label={`Rate ${s} star${s !== 1 ? "s" : ""}`}`** + **focus-visible ring ONLY** (whileTap present → CSS `active:scale` is DEAD, motion's inline transform overrides it; kept `transition-colors` for the hover bg/icon fade — the ring appears instantly on keyboard focus, a11y-correct); **(6) header back `motion.button`** (`whileTap={{ scale: 0.9 }}`, `onClick={() => navigate("/grocery")}`, icon-only `<ArrowLeft>`) → added **`aria-label="Go back"`** + ring ONLY; **(7) header refresh `motion.button`** (`whileTap={{ scale: 0.9 }}`, already had `aria-label="Refresh"`) → ring ONLY, no other change; **(8) filter tabs `motion.button` ×3** (`whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}`, visible text All/Active/Past → no aria-label) → added **`aria-pressed={filter === tab.key}`** (the active-tab selection was conveyed **only** visually via `bg-ig-gradient` → `aria-pressed` is a display-only attribute, same category as `aria-expanded` on #1, that lets screen readers announce "All, pressed" vs "Active, not pressed"; both advisors confirmed in-scope, NOT scope creep — the toggle-button pattern is correct here since these filter a list, not switch tabpanels) + focus-visible ring (className already had `transition-all`). **Edit-shape rule honored throughout:** raw `<button>`/`<a>` (#1–4) → full token set incl. live `active:scale`; `motion.button`s with `whileTap` (#5–8) → **ring ONLY** (no `active:scale` added — it'd be dead CSS under the inline motion transform); the 4 shadcn `<Button>`s (Track Order / Reorder / Help / Browse Stores) already ship tokens → **untouched**. **HARD RULE honored — every `supabase.select("*")` / the realtime channel `grocery-orders-realtime` / the `functions.invoke("shopping-order-state-update", …)` (auto-cancel + rate) / `cart.addItem` reorder / `STATUS_CONFIG`/`STEP_LABELS`/the `stats` useMemo/`filter`/`visibleOrders`/`activeCount` logic / `useState`/`useEffect` / the dynamic `import("@/lib/openExternalUrl")` / all `navigate` targets left byte-identical**: `git diff` = exactly **13 insertions / 8 deletions** (8 className swaps + 5 new standalone aria-attribute lines: copy `aria-label`, driver `aria-label`, star `aria-label`, back `aria-label`, tabs `aria-pressed`), and a guard grep over the diff (`onClick`/`navigate`/`supabase`/`select`/`channel`/`functions.invoke`/`cart.addItem`/`STATUS_CONFIG`/`STEP_LABELS`/`useState`/`useEffect`/`useMemo`/`filter`/`visibleOrders`/`openExternalUrl`/`setExpanded`/`setFilter`/`onRate`/`handleRefresh`/`copyOrderId`) surfaced only the expand `<button>` line (showing `onClick={() => setExpanded(!expanded)}` **byte-identical** on both `-`/`+` sides, only aria/className added) and the new `aria-pressed={filter === tab.key}` line (a display-only attr that merely reads `filter` state) → **CLEAN**. - **PublicDocumentView premium + responsive pass** (`src/pages/PublicDocumentView.tsx`, 287 lines, route `/d/:token`) — the customer-facing **shared invoice/estimate document** view (resolves an opaque share token via the `get_shared_document` security-definer RPC, then fetches `store_profiles` for the letterhead; renders an auto-repair invoice/estimate — store header, Bill-To/Vehicle block, a 4-column line-item table, totals, notes — with Print + Download-PDF actions). **Interaction-token audit: ZERO raw interactive controls** — the page's only two controls are the **Print** `<Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={window.print}>` (~L179) and the **Download PDF** `<Button size="sm" onClick={handleDownload}>` (~L182), **both shadcn** (already ship `active:scale-[0.98]` + `transition-all` + focus ring); everything else (the `<Card>`/`<CardContent>`, the invoice `<table>`/`<tr>`/`<td>`/`<th>`, every `<div>`/`<p>`/`<h1>`/`<h2>`/`<span>`, the loading `<Loader2>`, the not-found `<Card>`) is non-interactive with **no `onClick`** → correctly gets **NOTHING**. So **no interaction-token edits were needed** — the page was already press-feedback/focus-ring compliant. **One responsive-hardening edit applied** (className-only): the **line-item table wrapper `<div>`** (~L221) → added `overflow-x-auto print:overflow-visible`. **Why:** the table is `<table className="w-full text-sm">` under the default `table-layout:auto`, where `w-full` is a *preferred* (not *max*) width — so a long unbreakable token in the Description cell can push the table past 100% and overflow at 375px. This is an **auto-repair** document whose line items include **part numbers** (long, hyphenated/space-free tokens), so the overflow risk is real for this domain; `overflow-x-auto` lets the table scroll instead of bleeding off-screen, and shows **no scrollbar unless content actually overflows** (zero cost for normal invoices). `print:overflow-visible` preserves print/PDF fidelity (the browser paginates naturally — no clipping on the printed page). **Flagged fetched-but-unused field** — `Resolved.expires_at` is typed + returned by the RPC but **never read on the client** (expiry is enforced **server-side** in the `get_shared_document` security-definer RPC); it's a structural return-type artifact, **skipped** (removing it would require an RPC change — HARD-RULE-protected). All `store_*` fields ARE consumed (they feed `generateDocumentPdf`); all `d.*` doc fields are rendered or passed to the `PdfDoc`. **HARD RULE honored — the `get_shared_document` RPC / the `store_profiles` select / the logo→dataURL canvas effect / `generateDocumentPdf`+`downloadPdf` / `window.print()` / routing left byte-identical**: `git diff` = exactly **1 insertion / 1 deletion**, a pure className swap on the table wrapper. Advisors DeepSeek + MiMo both consulted (`docs/agent-runs/slice15-public-document-view-{deepseek,mimo}.md`); **both fully converged** that there are **zero raw interactive controls** (the 2 shadcn Buttons are the entire interactive surface) and that `expires_at` is the only fetched-but-unused field. **Divergence on the table — resolved toward the fix:** DeepSeek recommended **skipping** `overflow-x-auto` (called the scrollbar "visual noise on a page that never needs it"); MiMo recommended **adding** it ("a zero-risk defensive className addition — the standard Tailwind responsive-table pattern"). Took **MiMo's add** because (a) DeepSeek's rationale is technically off — `overflow-x-auto` renders **no** scrollbar unless overflow actually occurs, so it is *not* visual noise; (b) the auto-repair part-number domain makes the overflow genuinely possible; and (c) I neutralized the only real downside (this is a print-critical financial document) with `print:overflow-visible`, which neither advisor proposed. **Verified** `npm run update` green (**true exit 0, unpiped** — type-check + worker type-check both pass since the build only runs on `&&`; `✓ built in 7.77s`; `dist/sw.js` freshly generated + PWA precache 18 entries (2550.89 KiB) emitted). _Preview caveat: the populated document is gated behind a valid, unexpired share token + a populated `store_profiles` row (empty local DB → the reachable state is the "Link unavailable" expired/revoked card, which has no interactive controls), so rendering the invoice table needs a real token; per the established preview-contention protocol (shared dev server contended by parallel agents) I relied on the green gate + the clean 1/1 className-only diff + the well-established Tailwind `overflow-x-auto` responsive-table pattern (no new token semantics introduced)._ — @claude, 2026-06-13

- **GroceryOrderPlaced premium interaction pass** (`src/pages/grocery/GroceryOrderPlaced.tsx`, route `/grocery/order-placed`) — second page of the **Grocery post-checkout flow** cluster (follows `GroceryOrderTracking`). The **LIVE** post-checkout confirmation page: a PartyPopper success burst, an Order-ID card with a copy button, an order-items preview strip, a delivery-address card, a 4-step "What's next" stepper, two ETA info cards, and an actions block. Entrance animations are framer-motion `motion.div`; the interactive controls are **one raw `<button>`** (copy-order-ID) + **four shadcn `<Button>`s** (Track My Order / Shop More / Home / Share). A **very clean page** — needed only **2 className/display-only-attr edits**: (1) the **copy-order-ID raw `<button>`** (`onClick={copyOrderId}`, `className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5 group"` — contains the truncated order-ID text + a `<Copy>` icon) → added `aria-label="Copy order ID"` + appended `rounded-md transition-all active:scale-[0.97] focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring` (the `rounded-md` gives the otherwise-paddingless inline button a clean ring boundary; **`aria-label` is CORRECT here — opposite of the items-toggle clobber case** — because the button's visible content is a **data value** (the hex order ID), not an action label, so without a label a screen reader announces a meaningless "A1B2C3D4 …, button"; the label names the *action* while the ID stays visible + is read from the adjacent "Order ID" label; **both advisors explicitly confirmed** this trade-off); (2) the **icon-only Share `<Button>`** (`<Share2/>` only, `onClick` opens `openShareToChat`) → added `aria-label="Share order"` (shadcn `<Button>` ships interaction tokens but **no accessible name** for icon-only usage → it was announcing just "button"; this was the **primary a11y gap**). **Edit-shape rule honored:** the copy control is a raw `<button>` (not motion) → CSS `active:scale` is live → full token set; the Share control is a shadcn `<Button>` that already ships `transition-all`/`active:scale-[0.98]`/ring → **only the missing `aria-label`** added, no token churn; the 3 text-bearing `<Button>`s (Track My Order / Shop More / Home) already have visible-text accessible names → **untouched**. **HARD RULE honored — every supabase `.select()`/`maybeSingle()`/the `shopping_orders`→`grocery_orders` fallback fetch / the `openShareToChat({...})` payload / all `navigate` targets (`/grocery/track/${orderId}`, `/grocery`, `/`) / `useState`/`useEffect`/`copyOrderId` clipboard logic / the `STEPS` array / the `itemCount` reduce / the ETA text left byte-identical**: `git diff` = exactly **2 insertions / 2 deletions**, both pure className/aria; a guard grep over the diff confirmed `onClick={copyOrderId}` + the `openShareToChat` payload lines are unchanged (the copy line only surfaced because the substring `onClick` appears in it — the handler itself is identical on both sides). Advisors DeepSeek + MiMo both consulted (`docs/agent-runs/slice13-orderplaced-{deepseek,mimo}.md`); **both fully converged with each other AND my own audit** — identical 2-edit punch list, both independently confirmed the `aria-label="Copy order ID"` trade-off (data-value not action-label), both `active:scale-[0.97]` for the small copy button (also the `JobPostingDetailPage` button value), both verified **all 5 `select` cols are rendered** (`store`→ID card+SEO+share, `total_amount`→ID card+share, `items`→strip+count+share, `delivery_address`+`customer_name`→address card → **zero unused fields**), both verified the actions row (Shop More flex-1 + Home flex-1 + Share px-4) **holds at 375px** (~128px per flex-1 button vs ~120px content → fits, no crush), both noted the `text-[8px]`/`text-[9px]` micro-labels are design-system, not regressions. **Sole divergence resolved:** DeepSeek appended `focus-visible:ring-offset-2`, MiMo did not — **dropped the offset** (off-standard for this marathon; the copy button's parent ID card is not `overflow-hidden` so a normal outward ring renders cleanly without offset). **Verified** `npm run update` green (**true exit 0, unpiped** — type-check + worker type-check both pass since the build only runs on `&&`; `✓ built in 7.70s`; `dist/sw.js` freshly generated + PWA precache 18 entries (2550.82 KiB) emitted). _Preview caveat: the Share button + items/address/ID cards are gated behind a resolved `shopping_orders`/`grocery_orders` row (empty local DB; the copy button itself renders behind just the `?id=` param), and per the established preview-contention protocol (shared myzivo-dev server contended by parallel agents — navigating it would yank their page) I relied on the green gate + the clean 2/2 className-only diff + the **byte-identical** raw-button `active:scale`+ring token pattern + the trivial display-only `aria-label` additions, all already verified **live** on prior slices._ — @claude, 2026-06-13

- **GroceryOrderTracking premium interaction pass** (`src/pages/grocery/GroceryOrderTracking.tsx`, route `/grocery/track/:orderId`) — first page of the **Grocery post-checkout flow** cluster (follows the `/grocery` `GroceryMarketplace` landing). The **LIVE** order-tracking page: realtime `shopping_orders` status via a Supabase `postgres_changes` channel (`order-track-${orderId}`), an animated 5-step delivery stepper (`STEPS` const), a driver card (`drivers_public` fetch), an ETA countdown (`formatEta`/`timeAgo`), a collapsible items list (`showItems`), plus cancel-order (`cancel-grocery-order` invoke) + download-receipt (`grocery-order-receipt` invoke) actions. Brought to parity with `JobPostingDetailPage` via **6 className/display-only-attr edits**: (1) **header back** `<button onClick={()=>navigate(-1)}>` → added `aria-label="Go back"` + `transition-colors`→`transition-all active:scale-95 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring`; (2) **header copy-order-ID** `<button onClick={copyId}>` → added `aria-label="Copy order ID"` + the same token swap; (3) **driver Message** icon-`<button>` (`<MessageSquare>` child, **no onClick — see owner flag**) → added `aria-label="Message driver (coming soon)"` + `transition-all active:scale-95` + ring; (4) **driver Phone** icon-`<button>` (`<Phone>` child, **no onClick — see owner flag**) → added `aria-label="Call driver (coming soon)"` + the same set; (5) **items disclosure toggle** `<button onClick={()=>setShowItems(v=>!v)}>` → added `aria-expanded={showItems}` + `transition-all active:scale-[0.99] focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:ring-inset` (**`ring-inset`** because the parent card is `overflow-hidden` — a normal outer ring would clip; **no `aria-label`** added — the button has rich visible text `{count} Items · $X.XX` and a label would clobber that accessible name, so only `aria-expanded` for disclosure state); (6) **info-card store cell** `<div ...text-center>` → added `min-w-0` **and** its `<p>{order.store}</p>` → added `truncate` (the **3-column `grid grid-cols-3` cell** needs `min-w-0` on the grid item — grid items default to `min-width:auto` and won't shrink below content, so `truncate` alone overflows a long store name at 375px; `min-w-0` on the cell + `truncate` on the child is the correct pair). **Edit-shape rule honored:** raw `<button>`s → full token set incl. live `active:scale`; the shadcn `<Button>`s (View My Orders / My Orders / Shop More / Download receipt / Cancel order / Keep order / Cancel and refund) already ship tokens → **not touched**. **HARD RULE honored — every supabase `.select()` col / the realtime channel subscription / the `cancel-grocery-order` + `grocery-order-receipt` invokes / all `navigate` targets / `useState`/handlers / the stepper + ETA logic left byte-identical**: `git diff` = exactly **8 insertions / 7 deletions** (edit 6 adds one class to the `<div>` line + one to the `<p>` line → net +1 line over the others' 1:1 swaps), every line a className/aria swap, and a guard grep over the diff for `onClick`/`navigate`/`.select`/`channel`/`invoke` lines returned **zero**. Advisors DeepSeek + MiMo both consulted (`docs/agent-runs/slice12-grocery-tracking-{deepseek,mimo}.md`); **4 divergences resolved** — took MiMo's `active:scale-95` over DeepSeek's too-tight `scale-90` for the driver buttons; took MiMo's `"(coming soon)"` aria-labels; took MiMo's `ring-inset` on the items toggle; **REJECTED** MiMo's extra `aria-label="Expand items"` on the items toggle (clobbers the visible text) and synthesized the truncate fix correctly (`min-w-0` on the **cell** + `truncate` on the `<p>` — DeepSeek put `min-w-0` on the grid container, MiMo gave `truncate` with no `min-w-0`; neither got both). **Verified** `npm run update` green (**true exit 0, unpiped** — type-check + worker type-check both pass since the build only runs on `&&`; `✓ built in 6.80s`; `dist/sw.js` freshly generated + PWA 18 precache entries emitted). _Preview caveat: the stepper, driver card, info-card + items list are all gated behind a real `shopping_orders` row (empty local DB → `/grocery/track/<uuid>` lands on the "Order not found" error state, which DID confirm the back-button + error layout live); per the established preview-contention protocol (shared myzivo-dev server was contended by a parallel share-pages agent HMR-editing `src/index.css`) I relied on the green gate + the clean 8/7 className-only diff + the **byte-identical** raw-button `active:scale`+ring + `ring-inset`-on-overflow-hidden token patterns already verified **live** on prior slices._ **Owner flags (need owner, can't be done as className edits):** (a) the **2 driver contact buttons** (Message + Phone) are **non-functional — no `onClick` handler**; wire them up (deep-link to chat via `driver_id`; a `tel:` link — note `drivers_public` exposes **no phone column**, so this needs an RPC or a new column); the `aria-label="…(coming soon)"` signals the pending state to screen readers in the meantime. (b) **4 fetched-but-unused fields** — `shopping_completed_at` (could timestamp the Shopping step), `cancelled_at` (could show "Cancelled Xm ago" in the hero), `driver.vehicle_type` (could supplement the vehicle model), `driver.current_lat`/`current_lng` (realtime location fetched but no map consumes it — future map-integration candidate); surfacing these needs JSX, out of scope for this className-only slice. — @claude, 2026-06-13

Brought to parity with `JobPostingDetailPage` via **a single className-only edit**: the "Back to {store.name}" `<Link>` (~L165) → appended `rounded-sm transition-all active:scale-[0.97] focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring` (upgrading the link from no-transition + plain `hover:text-foreground` to the standard nav-link interaction token set). **No other raw controls exist** — Subscribe / "Not available yet" / Cancel / "Continue to Stripe" are all shadcn `<Button>`s and the email/name fields are shadcn `<Input>`/`<Label>` (already ship `active:scale` + ring); the loading (`Loader2`) and error ("Salon not found") states are pure text. **Tier `<Card>` (~L194) deliberately gets NOTHING** — it has **no `onClick`** (the "selected" ring is driven by the inner Subscribe `<Button>`, not card-click), so it's a non-focusable, non-interactive `<div>`: a focus ring there is dead CSS and `active:scale` on a non-interactive card is wrong. Both advisors independently confirmed this skip. **No fetched-but-unused fields** — every `Tier` field (`id` key+selection / `name` / `description` / `monthly_price_cents` / `billing_interval` / `service_discount_percent` / `has_stripe_price` disabled-gate) and every `StoreLite` field (`id` RPC param / `name` heading+empty-state / `slug` back-link) is rendered or consumed. **HARD RULE honored — the `store_profiles` fetch / the `salon_public_get_membership_tiers` RPC / the `subscribe-salon-membership` invoke / routing / the `subscribe()` + status-param logic left byte-identical**: `git diff` = exactly **1 insertion / 1 deletion**, a pure className swap. Advisors DeepSeek + MiMo both consulted (`docs/agent-runs/slice9-salon-membership-{deepseek,mimo}.md`); both fully converged on the identical single back-link edit, both confirmed the tier-Card skip (no `onClick`), both confirmed no other raw controls + all fields used + no responsive crush (single `max-w-md px-4` centered column, tier list `space-y-3`, the Cancel/Continue `flex gap-2` row holds at 375px). **Verified** `npm run update` green (exit 0, unpiped; type-check + worker type-check pass, PWA `dist/sw.js` + 18 precache entries). _Preview caveat: at verification time both full-app dev servers (myzivo-dev 5174 on `/car-rental-booking/…`, dev 8090 on `/car-dealership/…/v/veh-1`) were actively driven by the parallel car-rental + car-dealership slices, and the third repo server (zivodriver 8080) runs a different app config that 404s on `/salon/:slug/membership` — so rather than fight for a contended preview (a fetch stub would pollute the parallel agents' pages) I relied on the green gate + the diff + the byte-identical back-link interaction pattern already verified **live** across prior slices (most directly the car-rental review back-link: `hasRing:true, active:scale-[0.97]:true, rounded-sm:true`). Card/wide controls use `active:scale-[0.98]`, chips `[0.97]`, icon-only (qty steppers, heart) `[0.95]`, full-width FAQ rows `[0.99]`; every **raw** `<button>` also gets `focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring` (FAQ rows use `ring-inset`). **Deliberately did NOT add rings to the shadcn `<Button>`s** (Apply/Back/Next/Submit/hero/bottom CTA) — they already ship focus rings, so they got `active:scale-[0.98] transition-transform` only. **Also surfaced 2 fetched-but-unused vehicle fields** (verified fetched via `select("*")`, display-only): `description` (line-clamp-2 under the title) and `mileage_limit_per_day` (a new `Gauge` spec chip "N km/day") on the wizard vehicle card. **One responsive fix:** the wizard fleet grid `sm:grid-cols-2` → `sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3` (matches the storefront grid). **HARD RULE honored — all supabase queries / mutations / react-query keys / the `create_car_rental_app_reservation` RPC + all `car_rental_*` fetches / routing / pricing + availability + promo logic left byte-identical** (className + 2 display-only conditionals + 1 `Gauge` import only). Result: the major overlays already gate on `isCurrentZivoTravelHost()` (`RouteAwareGlobalUI` → CookieConsent/PWA prompts/InAppBrowser interstitial; `DeferredPassiveChatOverlays` → chat reaction overlays; `DesktopNavBootstrap` → GlobalDesktopNav). One real leak found + fixed: `src/components/app/NavigationProgressBar.tsx` (the 2px top route-change bar) used `bg-ig-gradient` (IG pink/purple) + pink glow on every navigation — now host-aware: sky→blue→cyan gradient + blue glow on the travel host, unchanged IG gradient on zivosmedia. It sits at the app root **outside** the `.zivo-travel-3d` scope, so the gradient is chosen by `isZivoTravelHost` rather than CSS inheritance. Kept the bar (useful feedback) instead of hiding it. Deliberately did NOT gate Toaster/Sonner — travel pages legitimately fire toasts. **Verified in preview** (computed style): social = `linear-gradient(45deg, …orange→red)` + pink glow; `?zt=1` travel = `linear-gradient(90deg, #0ea5e9→#3b82f6→#22d3ee)` + blue glow. `npm run update` green. — @claude, 2026-06-12
- **Travel-host mobile bottom nav fixed** — `src/components/app/ZivoMobileNav.tsx` (the shared mobile pill nav, mounted on 60+ pages) showed the **social** tabs (Home/Feed/Reels/Ride/Chat/Account) on the travel host's booking/landing pages (`/hotels`, `/cars`, `/car-rental`, `/flights`, `/bus` — all `isZivoTravelPath`, all mount `MobileBottomNav`). Now, when `isZivoTravelHost()` is true (zivostravel.com **or** the `?zt=1` preview flag), it renders a **travel** tab set — Home / Trips / Wallet / Cards / Account (Compass/Luggage/Wallet/CreditCard/UserRound) pointing at `/`, `/my-trips`, `/wallet`, `/payment-methods`, `/account` — mirroring the `TravelUtilityShell` bottom nav so the whole travel host has ONE consistent nav. Social host unchanged (gate is host-scoped; verified social tabs still render with the flag cleared).
